# Classification at `41 <= n <= 48`: every `S_k` class, every covered check rank

**PARTIAL BUILD** -- sources not yet swept: `rm37_w48`; sources included with coverage gaps: `length48`.

- `length48`: 62998 of 15566440 geometries are NOT classified: 62931 in 2 shard(s) with no result file (211 representatives) and 67 geometries the engine gave up on (listed).  Every other geometry is classified completely; the catalogue is exact on those and silent on these.
  - shard 314: 164 representatives (`length48_m9_class_02339` .. `length48_m8_class_00080`), 49828 geometries, not run
  - shard 315: 47 representatives (`length48_m8_class_00082` .. `length48_m8_class_00026`), 13103 geometries, not run
  - 67 failed geometries, by (n, check rank): n=48, rank 8: 67; by error: 67 x `OrbitTooLarge: k=6 orbit exceeds 150000 members`

**4207 distinct `(n, k, S_k gate)` classes** of distance-3 factory with 41 to 48 injections, each with the witness circuit of smallest leading error coefficient and the best witness at every check rank the class occurs at.  `a3` is the number of 3-sets of injections that pass every check and act on the outputs: `P_fail ~ a3 p^3`; `a3 = 0` means the witness has distance 4 (`P_fail = O(p^4)`), and the `[[n,k,d]]` label carries the distance of the primary witness, the largest over its ranks (a rank whose best witness has a smaller distance is marked in the last column).  1267 classes occur at more than one check rank; for 420 of the 4207 the best circuit is not the fewest-check one.

## What is covered

A distance-3 factory with n injections has a check parent of some rank r; the parent's columns plus (n odd) the origin form a no-repeated-column unital triorthogonal space of length n + (n mod 2).  The complete affine classifications of those spaces at lengths 42, 44, 46, 48 (`length*_catalogue.json`) marked at every origin give every parent -- except that the length-48 table's affine-rank-7 sector is missing two classes (`docs/INPUT_DEFECT_M7.md`), so the whole rank-7 sector at n = 47, 48 is taken from the RM(3,7) orbit table instead (unconditional), and the one affine-rank-6 word of weight 48 gives parents no brute-force engine can enumerate (`docs/OPEN_RANK6.md`).

| source | n | check ranks | representatives | geometries | max kappa | max width | classes | status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `length42` | 41, 42 | all | 86 | 41558 | 8 | 5 | 155 | complete |
| `length44` | 43, 44 | >= 8 | 508 | 239100 | 12 | 6 | 345 | complete |
| `rm37_w44` | 43, 44 | 7, 8 | 35 | 474 | 12 | 7 | 835 | complete |
| `length46` | 45, 46 | all | 1015 | 326647 | 10 | 7 | 764 | complete |
| `length48` | 47, 48 | >= 8 | 28776 | 15503442 | 10 | 7 | 2453 | **partial**: 62998 of 15566440 geometries not classified (62931 in 2 unswept shard(s), 67 orbit-cap failures; listed in the JSON) |
| `rm37_w48` | 47, 48 | 7, 8 | | | | | | **not swept** |
| *(open)* | 47, 48 | 6, and the rank-7 lift at n = 48 | 1 | 3 | 20–21 | <= 15 | ? | **open** |

In total 16111221 check parents were classified completely (every compatible subspace at every width, no budget, no cap; `classify48.py`); the sources marked partial above have further parents that are not classified, and a class carried only by those parents would be absent here.  n = 47, 48 from the unique affine-rank-6 weight-48 word (RM(3,7) class 3470, a+abc): check rank 6 at n = 47 and 48, and its rank-7 lift at n = 48.  Not swept: kappa = 20, 21, frames up to width 15 possible.  See docs/OPEN_RANK6.md.
